     <section class="MuiBox-root css-1k9ek97" id="section-id-134527504">
      <div class="MuiContainer-root MuiContainer-maxWidthXl css-8fzy2p">
       <div class="MuiBox-root css-1939klg">
        <p class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 css-1enhg8d">
         <strong>
          What is a vulnerability disclosure program?
         </strong>
        </p>
        <p class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 css-1enhg8d">
         A
         <a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways css-23qf9t" href="https://go.intigriti.com/vdp" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">
          vulnerability disclosure program
         </a>
         (VDP) enables businesses to mitigate security risks by providing support for the coordinated disclosure of vulnerabilities before they’re exploited by cybercriminals. By hosting your VDP with Intigriti, you remove the headache of managing and triaging any incoming reports from ethical hackers.
        </p>
        <p class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 css-1enhg8d">
         <strong>
          What is the difference between a penetration test and a bug bounty?
         </strong>
        </p>
        <p class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 css-1enhg8d">
         These two services are suited to different needs. A traditional pentest is a great way to test the security of your assets, although they tend to be time-boxed exercises that can also be extremely expensive. A
         <a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways css-23qf9t" href="https://www.intigriti.com/landing/bug-bounty" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">
          bug bounty
         </a>
         , meanwhile, gives continuous security protection over time, where the bounties are open to a wide cross-section of ethical hackers.
        </p>
        <p class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 css-1enhg8d">
         <strong>
          How does a Hybrid Pentest work?
         </strong>
        </p>
        <p class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 css-1enhg8d">
         For those in search of some middle ground between a penetration test and a bug bounty, Intigriti’s
         <a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways css-23qf9t" href="https://www.intigriti.com/landing/hybrid-pentesting" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">
          Hybrid Pentest
         </a>
         is perfect for fast, targeted checks on assets. These time-bound pentests use expert members of our hacking community. This SaaS-based approach to vulnerability assessment and penetration testing marks a new chapter in enterprise cybersecurity.
        </p>
        <p class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 css-1enhg8d">
         <strong>
          What is a Live Hacking Event?
         </strong>
        </p>
        <p class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 css-1enhg8d">
         A
         <a class="MuiTypography-root MuiTypography-body1 MuiLink-root MuiLink-underlineAlways css-23qf9t" href="https://www.intigriti.com/landing/live-hacking-events" rel="noopener noreferrer" target="_blank">
          Live Hacking Event
         </a>
         allows ethical hackers to gather in person and collaborate to complete an intensive inspection of an asset. These high-profile events are ideal for testing mature security assets and are an excellent way to showcase your company’s commitment to cybersecurity.
        </p>
       </div>
      </div>
     </section>
